## 154. ShaSa — 2015-04-10 05:37:39

श्रद्धाभक्तिसमायुक्ताः 

*मूलम्- **mūlam-*

श्रद्धाभक्तिसमायुक्ता नान्यकार्येषु लालसाः।

वाग्यताः शुचयश्चैव श्रोतारः पुण्यशालिनः॥

śraddhābhaktisamāyuktā nānyakāryeṣu lālasāḥ।

vāgyatā: śucayaścaiva śrotāraḥ puṇyaśālinaḥ॥

*पदविभागः- **padavibhāgaḥ-** अन्वयः- **anvayaḥ-*
श्रद्धा–भक्ति–समायुक्ताः न अन्यकार्येषु लालसा: वाग्यता: शुचयः च एव श्रोतार: 
पुण्यशालिन:॥

śraddhā–bhakti–samāyuktāḥ na anyakāryeṣu lālasā: vāgyatā: śucayaḥ ca eva 
śrotāra: puṇyaśālina:॥

*प्रतिपदार्थः- **pratipadārthaḥ-*

श्रद्धा–भक्ति–समायुक्ताः = śraddhā–bhakti–samāyuktāḥ = endowed with श्रद्धा and 
devotion (श्रद्धा =intranslatable word; It is- devotedness, preparedness, 
trust in śāstra, reverence for guru, belief in system, confidence in self- 
all combined together. 

अन्यकार्येषु = na anyakāryeṣu = in any other works

न लालसा: = lālasā: = not absorbed in

वाग्यता: = vāgyatā: = of controlled, restrained speech

शुचयः च एव = śucayaḥ ca eva = of clean mind and thought, pure, upright

श्रोतार: = śrotāra: = listeners, audience

पुण्यशालिन: = puṇyaśālina: = filled with (religious or moral) merit, 
virtuous

*तात्पर्यम्- **tātparyam-*

Virtuous listeners are those who possess reverence and devotion, whose 
minds are not taking pleasure in other things, who are of controlled 
speech, and are pure.

Not found in [Böhtlingk's–Sprüche-1873-iii]

## 155. ShaSa — 2015-04-12 09:16:19

सम्पूर्णकुम्भो न करोति 

*मूलम्- **mūlam-*

सम्पूर्णकुम्भो न करोति शब्दं अर्धो घटो घोषमुपैति नूनं । (पाठ. घोषमुपैति 
सम्यक्)

विद्वान्‌ कुलीनो न करोति गर्वं मूढास्तु जल्पन्ति गुणैर्विहीनाः॥ (पाठ. 
विद्वान्नरो यो) (पाठ. गुणैर्विहीना बहु वादयन्ति)

sampūrṇakumbho na karoti śabdaṃ ardho ghaṭo ghoṣamupaiti nūnaṃ । (pāṭha. ghoṣamupaiti 
samyak)

vidvān‌ kulīno na karoti garvaṃ mūḍhāstu jalpanti guṇairvihīnāḥ॥ (pāṭha. 
vidvānnaro yo) (pāṭha. guṇairvihīnā bahu vādayanti)

*पदविभागः- **padavibhāgaḥ-*

सम्पूर्णकुम्भः न करोति शब्दं अर्धः घटः घोषम् उपैति नूनं । विद्वान्‌ कुलीनः 
न करोति गर्वं मूढाः तु जल्पन्ति गुणैः विहीनाः॥

sampūrṇakumbhaḥ na karoti śabdaṃ ardhaḥ ghaṭaḥ ghoṣam upaiti nūnaṃ । 
vidvān‌ kulīnaḥ na karoti garvaṃ mūḍhāḥ tu jalpanti guṇaiḥ vihīnāḥ॥

*अन्वयः- **anvayaḥ-*
सम्पूर्णकुम्भः शब्दं न करोति। अर्धः घटः नूनं घोषम् उपैति। विद्वान्‌ कुलीनः 
गर्वं न करोति। मूढाः तु गुणैः विहीनाः जल्पन्ति ॥

sampūrṇakumbhaḥ śabdaṃ na karoti। ardhaḥ ghaṭaḥ nūnaṃ ghoṣam upaiti। 
vidvān‌ kulīnaḥ garvaṃ na karoti। mūḍhāḥ tu guṇaiḥ vihīnāḥ jalpanti ॥

*प्रतिपदार्थः- **pratipadārthaḥ-*

सम्पूर्णकुम्भः = sampūrṇakumbhaḥ = a full (=filled) pot

शब्दं = śabdaṃ = sound

न करोति = na karoti = does not do

अर्धः = ardhaḥ = half(-filled)

घटः = ghaṭaḥ = pot

नूनं घोषम् = nūnaṃ ghoṣam = very much noise

उपैति = upaiti = makes (lit. under goes)

विद्वान्‌ = vidvān‌ = learned scholar

कुलीनः = kulīnaḥ = of noble family

गर्वं = garvaṃ = pride, haughtiness

न करोति = na karoti = does not do (here- become)

मूढाः तु = mūḍhāḥ tu = fools, block-heads

गुणैः = guṇaiḥ = by good qualities

विहीनाः = vihīnāḥ = devoid of, destitute or deprived of

जल्पन्ति = jalpanti = babble, prattle

*तात्पर्यम्- **tātparyam-*

A completely filled pot does not make sound. A half filled pot makes lot of 
noise. A scholar of good family does not become haughty. Only the foolish, 
bereft of good qualities, babble. 

Böhtlingk's–Sprüche-IIed. सुभाषित no. 6882., Printed Book Pg. 466, PDF Pg. 
239 [Böhtlingk's–Sprüche-1873-iii]

6882. Ein voller Topf macht keinen Lärm, ein halbvoller Krug bullert ganz 
gehörig: ein gelehrter Mann zeigt keinen Hochmuth; wer aber der Vorzüge 
ermangelt, der macht viel Lärm von sich.

## 156. ShaSa — 2015-04-13 06:11:53

गुणवज्जनसंसर्गात् याति 

*मूलम्- **mūlam-*

गुणवज्जनसंसर्गात् याति नीचोऽपि गौरवम्।

पुष्पमालाप्रसङ्गेन सूत्रं शिरसि धार्यते॥

guṇavajjanasaṃsargāt yāti nīco'pi gauravam।

puṣpamālāprasaṅgena sūtram śirasi dhāryate॥

*पदविभागः- **padavibhāgaḥ-*

गुणवत् जनसंसर्गात् याति नीचः अपि गौरवम्। पुष्पमाला–प्रसङ्गेन सूत्रम् शिरसि 
धार्यते॥

guṇavat janasaṃsargāt yāti nīcaḥ api gauravam। puṣpamālā–prasaṅgena sūtram 
śirasi dhāryate॥

*अन्वयः- **anvayaḥ-*
गुणवत् जनसंसर्गात् नीचः अपि गौरवम् याति। पुष्पमाला–प्रसङ्गेन सूत्रम् शिरसि 
धार्यते॥

guṇavat janasaṃsargāt nīcaḥ api gauravam yāti। puṣpamālā–prasaṅgena sūtram 
śirasi dhāryate॥

*प्रतिपदार्थः- **pratipadārthaḥ-*

गुणवत् = guṇavat = having good qualities

जनसंसर्गात् = janasaṃsargāt = by being in company of, keeping in contact of 
(such) people/person

नीचः अपि = nīcaḥ api = even a lowly person

गौरवम् = gauravam = respect

याति = yāti = reaches, attains

पुष्पमाला–प्रसङ्गेन = puṣpamālā–prasaṅgena = by having contact with garland

सूत्रम् = sūtram = thread

शिरसि = śirasi = on head

धार्यते = dhāryate = is kept, is worn

*तात्पर्यम्- **tātparyam-*

By the company of virtuous people, even a lowly person attains respect. The 
thread, due to its association with the flowers, is worn on the head 
[respected]. [It’s a popular tradition among women to wear flowers to 
decorate hair.]

Böhtlingk's–Sprüche-IIed. सुभाषित-सं. 2120. (841), Printed Book Pg. 415, PDF 
Pg. 217

2120. (841.) Durch Verkehr mit Vorzüglichen Menschen erhält sogar ein 
Unbedeutender Ansehen: ein Faden kommt hoch auf’s Haupt zu stehen durch 
seinen Anschluss an einen Blumenkranz.  [Böhtlingk's–Sprüche-1870-i]

## 157. ShaSa — 2015-04-14 07:11:13

शब्दजालं महारण्यं 

*मूलम्- **mūlam-*

शब्दजालं महारण्यं चित्तभ्रमणकारणम् ।

अतः प्रयत्नाज्ज्ञातव्यं तत्त्वज्ञात्तत्त्वमात्मनः ॥ विवेकचूडामणिः, ६०

śabdajālaṃ mahāraṇyaṃ cittabhramaṇakāraṇam ।

ataḥ prayatnājjñātavyaṃ tattvajñāttattvamātmanaḥ ॥ vivekacūḍāmaṇiḥ 60

*पदविभागः- **padavibhāgaḥ-*

शब्दजालं महारण्यं चित्तभ्रमणकारणम् अतः प्रयत्नात् ज्ञातव्यं तत्त्वज्ञात् 
तत्त्वम् आत्मनः ।

śabdajālaṃ mahāraṇyaṃ cittabhramaṇakāraṇam ataḥ prayatnāt jñātavyaṃ 
tattvajñāt tattvam ātmanaḥ ।

*अन्वयः- **anvayaḥ-*
शब्दजालं महारण्यं चित्तभ्रमणकारणम् । अतः तत्त्वज्ञात् प्रयत्नात् आत्मनः 
तत्त्वं ज्ञातव्यम् ।

śabdajālaṃ mahāraṇyaṃ cittabhramaṇakāraṇam । ataḥ tattvajñāt prayatnāt 
ātmanaḥ tattvaṃ jñātavyam ।

*प्रतिपदार्थः- **pratipadārthaḥ-*

शब्दजालं = śabdajālaṃ = a collection, mass of words

महारण्यं = mahāraṇyaṃ = great forest

चित्तभ्रमणकारणम् = cittabhramaṇakāraṇam = cause of confusion in mind

अतः = ataḥ = hence, therefore

तत्त्वज्ञात् = tattvajñāt = from the one who knows the truth (about Self)

प्रयत्नात् = prayatnāt = through effort 

आत्मनः = ātmanaḥ = of the Self

तत्त्वं = tattvaṃ = Truth

ज्ञातव्यम् = jñātavyam = should know, learn

*तात्पर्यम्- **tātparyam-*

Volumes of [scriptural] words are like a dense forest and can cause mind to 
deviate/confuse. Therefore one must study about Self Knowledge from a 
Self-realized person.

[Not found in Böhtlingk's–Sprüche-1873iii]

## 158. ShaSa — 2015-04-15 05:41:43

वदनं प्रसादसदनं 

*मूलम्- **mūlam-*

वदनं प्रसादसदनं सदयं हृदयं सुधामुचो वाचः ।

करणं परोपकरणं येषां केषां न ते वन्द्याः ॥

vadanaṃ prasādasadanaṃ sadayaṃ hṛdayaṃ sudhāmuco vācaḥ ।

karaṇaṃ paropakaraṇaṃ yeṣāṃ keṣāṃ na te vandyāḥ ॥

*पदविभागः- **padavibhāgaḥ-*

वदनं प्रसादसदनं सदयं हृदयं सुधामुचः वाचः । करणं परोपकरणं येषां केषां न ते 
वन्द्याः ॥

vadanaṃ prasādasadanaṃ sadayaṃ hṛdayaṃ sudhāmucaḥ vācaḥ ।karaṇaṃ 
paropakaraṇaṃ yeṣāṃ keṣāṃ na te vandyāḥ ॥

*अन्वयः- **anvayaḥ-*
वदनं प्रसादसदनं सदयं हृदयं सुधामुचः वाचः । करणं परोपकरणं येषां ते केषां न 
वन्द्याः ॥

vadanaṃ prasādasadanaṃ sadayaṃ hṛdayaṃ sudhāmucaḥ vācaḥ ।karaṇaṃ 
paropakaraṇaṃ yeṣāṃ te keṣāṃ na vandyāḥ ॥

*प्रतिपदार्थः- **pratipadārthaḥ-*

वदनं = vadanaṃ = face

प्रसादसदनं = prasādasadanaṃ = abode of happiness

सदयं = sadayaṃ = compassionate, kind

हृदयं = hṛdayaṃ = heart

सुधामुचः = sudhāmucaḥ = releasing (giving out) nectar, expressing sweetness

वाचः = vācaḥ = words

करणं = karaṇaṃ = deed, act, action.

परोपकरणं = paropakaraṇaṃ = helping or doing good to others [Service, help, 
assistance, favour, kindness, obligation to others]

येषां = yeṣāṃ = of whom

ते = te = they

केषां = keṣāṃ = whose

न वन्द्याः = na vandyāḥ = respectable, adorable

Who will not adore those, whose face is abode of happiness, words are 
nectar-like (sweet),  acts are to help others? [Lit. to whom are they 
adorable not?]

 [Not found in Böhtlingk's–Sprüche-1873iii]

## 159. ShaSa — 2015-04-15 06:13:50

Arvind sir had asked if this was the first thread of subhAShitas. (Starting 
with चिन्तायाश्च...)


No sir. The subhAShitas actually were begun in the first thread (link 
below), and continued in the second one- then took up this (present) thread 
based on changes made in presentation.

Here are links-


*https://groups.google.com/forum/#!topic/nagari/omIjr0RO5t0 
<https://groups.google.com/forum/#!topic/nagari/omIjr0RO5t0>*

*https://groups.google.com/forum/#!topic/nagari/rpR8d1BW1jE 
<https://groups.google.com/forum/#!topic/nagari/rpR8d1BW1jE>*

introducing questions-answers session.. with name -सुभाषितप्रश्नावलिः 

https://groups.google.com/forum/#!topic/nagari/sym6K4A1wl4

But due to only a single response, it was closed.


The number of subhAShitas has been decided as 108.
Until now 107 have been done. I am happy to present the last closing 
subhAShita tomorrow.

This question - answer session can be continued if readers and students of 
संस्कृत in this group will come up with more responses. Please see this 
thread and (https://groups.google.com/forum/#!topic/nagari/sym6K4A1wl4) let 
me know if any changes are needed in the questioning way. It can also be 
made one per week. 
Otherwise, the session will come to an end tomorrow.

Thank you so much for all the discussions. Thanks to Serge for coming up 
with Russian translations of these wonderful sayings. Hope he comes up with 
translations of the remaining ones as well. 
Thank you

## 160. Arvind_Kolhatkar — 2015-04-15 15:53:14

Friends, 

I have a suggestion to offer.

With multiple subhaaShitas and over 160 responses on a single page, the 
page becomes too long and confused, especially when in a response, all 
previous responses get automatically included below.

A solution would be to conclude a thread after a fixed number of responses, 
say 50 or 100, and open a serially numbered new thread with the same 
heading as 'सुभाषितदैनंदिनम् १, सुभाषितदैनंदिनम् २, etc., including in each 
of them links to its predecessors.  After a sufficient number of such 
threads, all the earlier subhaaShitas could be included as bare texts in a 
special thread, with cross-references to where there detailed expositions 
can be found.

Over a long term this could be an easily searchable anthology of 
subhaaShitas .

Arvind Kolhatkar.

## 163. ShaSa — 2015-04-17 08:53:45

Namaste
Here is the closing one in the long subhAShita series. The series started 
with one- उद्यमेन हि सिद्ध्यन्ति which was my favourite shloka, and this one
क्षणशः कणशश्चैव is equally inspiring shloka in my case. subhAShitas have 
been inspiring me since the commencement of my संस्कृताध्ययनं.. They 
motivated me; they lead me when I knew not the way; they filled me with 
strength whenever I needed it. Great poets and writers all through the way 
of humanity's life journey, have spoken for its good, betterment.., with 
all their expertise and experience. They helped us learn without taking 
pains ourselves.
So I dedicate this effort - to the holy feet of all those noble humans.

The whole list of all the subhAshitas done will be presented soon.
Thank you to each of the members who helped me by their advises, directions 
and corrections.
If this effort helps at least one member in the group to improve their 
संस्कृतज्ञानं then I feel happy, that my time was not spent in vain. 
With lot of good feelings- अत्र विरमामि। शुभमस्तु।

*क्षणशः कणशश्चैव * 

*मूलम्- **mūlam-*

क्षणशः कणशश्चैव विद्यामर्थं च साधयेत् ।

क्षणे नष्टे कुतो विद्या कणे नष्टे कुतो धनम् ॥

kṣaṇaśaḥ kaṇaśaścaiva vidyāmarthaṃ ca sādhayet ।

kṣaṇe naṣṭe kuto vidyā kaṇe naṣṭe kuto dhanam ॥

*पदविभागः- **padavibhāgaḥ-*

क्षणशः कणशः च एव विद्याम् अर्थं च साधयेत् । क्षणे नष्टे कुतः विद्या कणे 
नष्टे कुतः धनम् ॥

kṣaṇaśaḥ kaṇaśaḥ ca eva vidyām arthaṃ ca sādhayet । kṣaṇe naṣṭe kutaḥ vidyā 
kaṇe naṣṭe kutaḥ dhanam ॥

*अन्वयः- **anvayaḥ-*
क्षणशः कणशः च एव विद्याम् अर्थं च साधयेत् । क्षणे नष्टे कुतः विद्या कणे 
नष्टे कुतः धनम् ॥

kṣaṇaśaḥ kaṇaśaḥ ca eva vidyām arthaṃ ca sādhayet । kṣaṇe naṣṭe kutaḥ vidyā 
kaṇe naṣṭe kutaḥ dhanam ॥

*प्रतिपदार्थः- **pratipadārthaḥ-*

क्षणशः = kṣaṇaśaḥ = from every moment (measure of time equal to 4/5 of a 
second)

कणशः च एव = kaṇaśaḥ ca eva = from every grain, a small bit of anything

विद्याम् = vidyām = knowledge

अर्थं च = arthaṃ ca = wealth, purpose

साधयेत् = sādhayet = should gain 

क्षणे नष्टे = kṣaṇe naṣṭe = when a moment is lost 

कुतः = kutaḥ = from where, whence

कणे नष्टे = kaṇe naṣṭe = when a grain is lost, [when even that] little bit 
is lost

धनम् = dhanam = money

*तात्पर्यम्- **tātparyam-*

One should learn from every moment, and earn wealth (or attain purpose) 
from each grain (every little bit). When time is lost, where is knowledge; 
and when grain is lost, where is money? (or when the little bit is lost, 
how is the purpose attained)?

Not found [Böhtlingk's–Sprüche-1870-i]

## 164. Arvind_Kolhatkar — 2015-04-17 19:06:35

I know another version of the same subhaaShita:

क्षणश: कणशश्चैव विद्यामर्थं च साधयेत्।
गृहीत इव केशेषु मृत्युना धर्ममाचरेत्॥

Knowledge and wealth should be collected (respectively) moment by moment 
and bit by bit.  धर्म (Good Order) should be adhered to as if grasped by 
the Death by the hair.

Arvind Kolhatkar.

## 165. Marcis — 2015-05-18 09:05:03

Изучение грамматики прославляется во многих санскритских строфах, пример 
тому:

yadyapi bahu nādhīṣe tathāpi paṭha putra vyākaraṇam |
svajanaḥ śvajano mābhut sakalaṁ śakalaṁ sakṛt śakṛt ||

«Даже если ты не изучаешь множество [дисциплин], учи, сынок, грамматику! 
Дабы родственник (svajanaḥ) не стал собакой (śvajanaḥ), целое (sakala) не 
стало частью (śakala), а «единожды» (sakṛt) не стало «испражнениями» 
(śakṛt)»*.

*замена одной шипящей на другую приводит к кардинальному изменению смысла.

Или («Раджа-нигханту», Грантха-праставана, 5):

nighaṇṭunā vinā vaidyo vidvān vyākaraṇaṃ vinā |
anabhyāsena dhānuṣkas trayo hāsyasya bhājanam ||

«Трое достойны осмеяния: врач без знания справочников о свойствах 
лекарственных средств, ученый без знания грамматики, лучник без постоянной 
практики».

https://www.facebook.com/ayurgol/posts/10153044602224821:3

On Friday, 17 April 2015 19:06:35 UTC+3, Arvind_Kolhatkar wrote:
>
> I know another version of the same subhaaShita:
>
> क्षणश: कणशश्चैव विद्यामर्थं च साधयेत्।
> गृहीत इव केशेषु मृत्युना धर्ममाचरेत्॥
>
> Knowledge and wealth should be collected (respectively) moment by moment 
> and bit by bit.  धर्म (Good Order) should be adhered to as if grasped by 
> the Death by the hair.
>
> Arvind Kolhatkar.
>
